# V5 Experiment Ledger

Pre-registration record for EVERY behavior-affecting change to the V5 trainer/env.
Rules: see `docs/V5_TRAINING_PLAYBOOK.md` §2. One experiment in its judgment window at a
time. Fill every field BEFORE the code runs. Statuses: REGISTERED → RUNNING →
ADOPTED | REVERTED | ABORTED (+ one-line lesson). Reporting-only changes do not go here.

Retroactive note: the three action-prior interventions already applied to the active run
(iter 4000 aprior 0.02, iter 4400 preprior 0.01, iter 4600 preprior 0.02 + call36)
predate this ledger. Their review artifacts live in
`reports/v5_75m_intervention_review.md` and the run dir. Post-hoc verdict per the
100M evidence: no measurable Slumbot gain (75M -71.5 → 100M -85.0, quick5k). They are
scheduled for removal under EXP-004.

---

## Template (copy for each new entry)

```
### EXP-NNN <short name>
- status: REGISTERED
- date registered / launched / judged:
- owner: <agent>
- problem: <audit ID S*/M* + one sentence>
- hypothesis: <what will improve and WHY>
- exact change: <flags / files / diff summary; continuation run-dir suffix>
- baseline: <checkpoint iter+hands archived at cutover; metric window recorded>
- expected effect: <metric + direction + magnitude>
- gate (pass criteria): <metric, threshold, sample size, window>
- abort criteria: <what triggers early revert>
- rollback: <checkpoint path + code path to restore>
- result / lesson: <fill at judgment>
```

---

## Seeded queue (from roadmap — register properly before launching each)

### EXP-001 Mirrored-deal internal evaluation harness (R7)
- status: ADOPTED
- date registered / launched / judged: registered `2026-07-06`; implementation window opened `2026-07-06 11:25 EDT`; 10k-pair gate artifact written `2026-07-06 11:53 EDT`; supplemental prior-anchor 25k artifact written and judged `2026-07-06 12:07 EDT`
- owner: Codex
- problem: M9 - no signal between Slumbot runs has usable resolution.
- hypothesis: 10k duplicate-deal pairs vs frozen anchors gives ~+/-8-15 bb/100 CI, enabling
  every later experiment to be judged cheaply.
- exact change: new script `scripts/alpha_holdem/v5_mirror_eval.py` (read-only vs frozen
  checkpoints; no trainer change). Anchors: `models/alpha_holdem_v4_final.pt` + best prior
  V5 checkpoint. Output: JSON/MD with bb/100 +/- CI per anchor.
- baseline: active V5 run at checkpoint iter/hands `8900` / `146,035,241`; latest official
  Slumbot remains 100M quick5k greedy `-85.037 bb/100`, CI `[-129.224, -40.851]`; no
  formal strength claim allowed. Harness will default to read-only evaluation of frozen
  checkpoints and will not touch trainer PID `56876`.
- expected effect: measurement capability only. The script must produce paired candidate
  minus anchor bb/100, 95% CI, seat-swap pair counts, and JSON/MD artifacts suitable for
  later EXP-002..008 gates.
- gate: measured CI <= +/-20 bb/100 at 10k pairs; V4-anchor result consistent with known
  V4<->V5 quick5k gap direction.
- abort: n/a (no trainer risk).
- rollback: n/a.
- result / lesson: implementation validation passed `py_compile`; self-vs-self mirrored
  smoke produced `0.0 bb/100`; initial 500-pair CPU artifact was written to
  `<run_dir>\v5_mirror_eval_exp001_500p.json/md`. Initial 500-pair result was not a gate:
  active 8900 vs V4 `-46.98 bb/100 +/-104.37`; active 8900 vs prior V5 59M `-72.24 bb/100
  +/-244.29`. The 10k-pair CPU artifact is
  `<run_dir>\v5_mirror_eval_exp001_10kp.json/md`: active 9000 vs V4 `-92.02 bb/100
  +/-13.31` over 10k pairs, and active 9000 vs prior V5 59M `+29.99 bb/100 +/-30.40`
  over 10k pairs. V4 anchor met the CI target; prior V5 needed more samples. Supplemental
  artifact `<run_dir>\v5_mirror_eval_exp001_priorv5_25kp.json/md` scored active 9000 vs
  prior V5 59M at `+27.98 bb/100 +/-18.61` over 25k pairs, meeting the CI target. EXP-001
  is adopted as the internal measuring stick; it is still internal-only and cannot support
  Slumbot/L5/L6 claims.

### EXP-001 follow-ups (required before mirror eval is used as an experiment gate)
- status: ADOPTED (script-only; 10k current-active gate PASS; original active_9000 replay not reproducible because its artifact pointed at mutable latest.pt)
- date: 2026-07-07 (from Fable 11-agent audit)
- exact change, three fixes to scripts/alpha_holdem/v5_mirror_eval.py:
  1. Anchor legality emulation: when an anchor checkpoint's env_version implies
     raise_cap_per_street=1 (v4 / v55cap1 / v55cap1v4obs), zero its raise/all-in mask
     slots in facing-bet postflop nodes so it never plays never-trained OOD actions;
     AND log ood_node_rate per run so drift stays visible.
  2. Tighten resolve_obs_version fallback to match play_slumbot.py (v5.5/v5.zero or
     explicit metadata only); legacy 'v5.0' must resolve to v4 obs.
  3. Record policy_mode ('greedy_argmax_both_sides') and per-side obs/env versions in
     the output JSON; remove the dead `swapped` parameter.
- gate: follow-up 10k-pair active-vs-V4 eval completed on a frozen gate_10900
  checkpoint after the script fixes: -69.32 +/-16.22 bb/100, CI target PASS, V4
  cap-1 emulation ON, anchor OOD rate 0.1983. This is compatible by overlapping CI
  with the adopted -92.02 +/-13.31 record, but it is not a strict replay of
  active_9000 because that checkpoint was not frozen.
- note: the adopted EXP-001 numbers remain valid as recorded; this hardens the stick
  before EXP-002..008 judgments depend on it. Future mirror gates must use frozen
  checkpoint copies, not mutable latest.pt paths.

### EXP-002 Batched multi-env rollout (R1)
- status: ADOPTED_RETAINED (cut over at gate 14000; throughput and health gates passed; retained through the active EXP-003 run)
- design blockers: RESOLVED in `reports/v5_exp002_multienv_design_20260706.md` on
  2026-07-07 before any trainer code landed.
  1. GAE trajectory-contiguity invariant is now explicit: multi-env workers must buffer
     per env and emit only completed hand packages. Each trainable trajectory sub-block
     is contiguous and done-terminated; each completed poker hand has exactly one
     `hand_marker=1.0`. Required assertion/test is specified.
  2. M=1 equivalence gate now uses chosen option (a), explicit per-worker seeding:
     pass deterministic worker/env seeds into both old one-env and candidate M=1 paths,
     seed Python random/NumPy/torch in workers, and require identical observations,
     masks, actions, rewards, committed-chip bounds, done positions, and hand accounting
     for at least 1,000 completed hands.
  3. Batching accumulation is specified: worker writes all pending M env requests before
     waiting; main waits for `inference_min_batch_slots` or a short deadline before
     inference. First scratch defaults proposed: M=16, min slots 256, deadline 500-1000us.
- date registered / launched / judged: seeded `2026-07-06`; formal baseline refreshed
  at gate `9100` / `149,316,834` hands on `2026-07-06 12:22 EDT`; launch pending; judged
  pending.
- owner: Codex
- problem: S1 - one env per worker and per-decision round trip leaves GPU mostly idle.
  Latest audit at iter 9103 shows effective h/s `596.8`, long-window h/s `455.0`,
  mean inference batch size `12.15`, PPO share `13.7%`, GPU utilization `14%`.
- hypothesis: running M envs per worker and batching their pending decisions will raise
  inference batch size from ~10-12 to 300+ and increase effective h/s by at least 2x
  without changing game semantics, hand accounting, opponent assignment semantics, or
  the official greedy Slumbot policy.
- exact change: implement a controlled batched multi-env rollout path in the trainer:
  each worker owns M v55 environments, steps each to its next decision, writes a W x M
  observation/request block, receives W x M action slots, and returns transitions with
  existing `hand_marker` actual-hand accounting. Add flags so the old one-env worker
  path remains selectable; do not change env_version, obs_version, action_space,
  reward semantics, policy network, PPO math, action priors, pool strategy, or Slumbot
  policy. Continuation suffix planned: `_exp002_multienv_r1`.
- baseline: active run checkpoint `9100` / `149,316,834`, path
  `<run_dir>\latest.pt`, fresh-from-zero `true`, health `PASS`, workers `22`,
  hands_per_iter `16384`, opponent_assignment `per-iteration`, pool_strategy
  `loss-kbest`; throughput audit `<run_dir>\v5_throughput_audit.json` checked at
  `2026-07-06T16:21:35Z` with tail_60 effective h/s `596.8`, tail_240 effective h/s
  `455.0`, mean inf_bs `12.15`, collect mean `25.11s`, PPO mean `3.60s`.
- expected effect: primary engineering metric >=2.0x effective h/s versus the registered
  baseline with candidate mean inf_bs >=300 if M is 16+; long-run target is reducing
  2.7B ETA from ~50-70 days toward ~10-30 days. This is not a strength claim.
- gate (pass criteria): after offline validation and controlled gate-boundary cutover,
  run `v5_throughput_compare.py --baseline-run-dir <current_run> --candidate-run-dir
  <exp002_run> --tail 20 --min-baseline-rows 20 --min-candidate-rows 20
  --min-hps-ratio 2.0 --min-candidate-inf-bs 300`; then require health `PASS` for 3
  post-cutover gates, no stderr incident, action-mix/rew100 within pre-registered
  guard bands, and EXP-001 mirrored internal eval non-regression beyond CI at the next
  snapshot. Next scheduled quick5k must not regress by >60 bb/100 versus the latest
  pre-change official quick screen.
- abort criteria: any fixed-seed M=1 equivalence mismatch in observation/legal/reward
  trace; candidate h/s ratio <1.2 after one tuning attempt; sustained health FAIL or
  crash traced to the change; incorrect actual-hand accounting; opponent assignment
  drift; queue/gate/checkpoint freshness failure.
- rollback: do not adopt the code path; continue from the archived pre-cutover checkpoint
  with the old one-env worker path and prior flags via `v5_continue_after_gate.ps1`.
  If already cut over, revert at the next gate boundary using the archived checkpoint and
  mark EXP-002 `REVERTED` with the failure mode.
- result / lesson: implementation package completed on 2026-07-07 and logged in Ops rows
  08:40-09:55; pinned `scripts/alpha_holdem/train_v5_exp002.py` sha256
  `16bad7b5645bd5a66077b3508fcf6142f8893760250c423d9bb5ef984dd668bc`. Evidence:
  `reports/exp002_evidence/` with self-play and opp-mode M=1 equivalence traces, GPU
  scratch A/B logs, and checksums. Cutover is NOT launched; it must wait for EXP-004
  step-1 judgment plus a PASS gate boundary, then follow the amended archive/copy/launch
  procedure in the 09:40 Ops row and judge with the amended inf_bs >=150 gate.

### EXP-003 Mirrored self-play deals + all-in runout EV (R3)
- status: RUNNING_JUDGMENT_PENDING (bounded-K=200 cutover at gate 21800 / 358,064,575; first eligible judgment checkpoint is PASS and >=408,064,575)
- problem: M1 — gradient dominated by card luck; vloss ~2000 at 145M hands.
- hypothesis: duplicate deals cancel luck in-iteration; all-in EV removes runout noise;
  critic converges visibly faster at matched hands.
- exact change: (a) self-play deals played twice with seats swapped; (b) all-in-before-river
  terminal reward = exact equity × pot (registered reward-semantics deviation).
- gate: vloss at matched hand counts clearly below pre-change trend; EXP-001 eval improves
  over ≥30M-hand window; next quick5k not worse by >60.
- abort: real-hands/sec drops >20% (remember the V5.0 both-player-collect trap), or eval
  regresses beyond CI.
- rollback: archived checkpoint + disable flags.

### EXP-004 Action-prior decay to zero (R6)
- status: ROLLED_BACK_TO_STABLE_FLOOR (0.005 step failed and was rolled back; retain preflop 0.01 / postflop 0.02; zero-prior step is not authorized while EXP-003 is open)
- problem: M4 — three stacked priors shape frequencies without hand-strength conditioning;
  evidence flat-to-negative.
- hypothesis: with EXP-003 variance reduction in place, removing priors lets EV decide
  frequencies; entropy can finally decline; no SB-open collapse.
- exact change: halve all prior coefs each 25M hands until <0.005 then 0 (controlled
  continuations at gate boundaries).
- gate: EXP-001 eval non-inferior over each decay step; SB open raise rate does not fall
  below 0.25 sustained; guardrail-warning oscillation dampens.
- abort: SB open limp > 0.5 sustained ≥ 2 gates AND eval regression beyond CI.
- rollback: restore previous coef at next gate.
- dependency: judge only after EXP-003 adopted (or explicitly re-register standalone).
- RE-REGISTERED STANDALONE 2026-07-07 (Fable audit) — priority raised above EXP-002/003:
  - new evidence: 3rd consecutive official quick5k decline during the prior window
    (75M -71.5 → 100M -85.0 → 150M -94.9); 150M greedy SB open collapsed to
    fold/call/raise/allin 0.008/0.978/0.014/0.000; the active global preflop prior
    (call target 0.36, coef 0.02) rewards call mass at EVERY preflop node including SB
    open, making it a plausible direct cause; hero_fold worsened to -230.7 bb/100.
  - execution: at next gate boundary, halve preflop prior coef 0.02 → 0.01; then halve
    again every ~15M hands until < 0.005, then 0. Postflop aprior follows one step behind.
    One coef change per continuation window; ops-log row per step.
  - gate per step: EXP-001 mirror eval (post-fix version) vs V4 anchor AND vs the
    pre-decay checkpoint — non-inferior beyond CI; SB open raise rate (greedy probe)
    must move UP or hold; entropy must not collapse (<0.3 sustained).
  - note: abort criterion "SB open limp > 0.5" is ALREADY breached under the prior
    (0.978), so limp rate rising cannot be an abort reason for removal — only mirror-eval
    regression + entropy collapse can abort the decay.
  - launch baseline: gate_11000 checkpoint iter/hands 11000 / 180,491,565; frozen copy
    `models/alpha_holdem_v5_from_zero/v5_zero_l6_fixedenv_20260703_1445_after4000_aprior002_r1_after4400_preprior001_r1_after4600_preprior002_call36_r1/v5_exp004_predecay_gate11000_checkpoint.pt`.
  - first continuation: `v5_zero_l6_exp004_pre001_r1_20260707`; only preflop prior coef
    changes to 0.01; all other trainer/env settings preserved from the source run.

### EXP-005 Group opponent assignment (R5)
- status: REGISTERED
- problem: M3 — 100% single-opponent iterations lurch the data distribution per update.
- exact change: `--opponent-assignment per-group`, G=4-5 groups, one forced self-play group.
- gate: internal-probe swing amplitude and vloss variance visibly reduced over 20M hands;
  h/s within 10% of baseline; EXP-001 eval non-inferior.
- abort: h/s drop >15%.
- rollback: flag back to per-iteration.

### EXP-006 KL early-stop + entropy schedule (+ optional value rescale) (R8)
- status: REGISTERED
- problem: M5/M6/M7 — kl 0.035-0.044 and clipfrac ~0.2 per update; bang-bang entropy;
  ±200 BB value scale.
- exact change: break PPO epochs at approx_kl>0.03; entropy_coef linear 0.05→0.01 over
  first 1B hands (floor boost retained); optional /200 value-target reparam with matching
  δ2/δ3 + GAE rescale.
- gate: kl into 0.01-0.02 band; entropy declines smoothly; EXP-001 eval non-inferior over
  ≥30M hands.
- abort: entropy collapse (<0.3 sustained) or eval regression.
- rollback: flags off.

### EXP-007 ELO-ranked opponent pool (R4)
- status: REGISTERED
- problem: M2 — loss-kbest is not a strength ranking.
- exact change: at each snapshot, round-robin candidate vs pool via mirrored deals
  (1-2k pairs each), ELO update, keep top-5. Pool format unchanged.
- gate: pool ELO table logged per snapshot; EXP-001 eval trend over ≥30M hands ≥ baseline.
- abort: snapshot-time stall >5 min per event.
- rollback: `--pool-strategy loss-kbest`.

### EXP-008 Engine + encoding fast path (R2)
- status: REGISTERED
- problem: S2/S3 — clone-per-action, O(N²) history replay, pickled pipes.
- exact change: in-place rollout apply; incremental (25,4,5) history tensor; 9-card deal;
  card-tensor caching; shm ring buffer.
- gate: MANDATORY fixed-seed byte-equivalence over ≥1,000 hands (playbook §5.2) BEFORE
  cutover; then h/s ratio ≥1.5 and health streak.
- abort: any equivalence mismatch (fix or drop; never adopt "close enough").
- rollback: old env import path.

### EXP-009 CFR warm-start validation (BC bootstrap branch) — USER-APPROVED 2026-07-07
- status: CLOSED_PHASE1_NO_GO_VALUE_TARGET_UNAVAILABLE
- owner: Codex
- authorization: user approved the VALIDATION on 2026-07-07 (via Fable). This validates
  a possible AlphaGo-style bootstrap; it does NOT yet authorize switching the main line.
  Switching remains a user decision taken on this experiment's data.
- problem: from-zero V5 spent 180M hands to reach -95 quick5k while the historical BC
  anchor (CFR-cloned, hours of GPU) reached ~V4-level (~-50). If a properly built BC
  model (policy AND value heads) lands near -50, bootstrapping buys ~hundreds of
  millions of hands. Known prior failure to fix: RL-1 died of value-head cold start
  (BC taught policy only; PPO's blind critic destroyed the policy).
- hard guards:
  1. The from-zero main run (v5_zero_l6_exp004_pre001_r1) keeps training untouched.
  2. BC branch artifacts live in a separate family `models/alpha_holdem_v5_bc_bootstrap/`
     with `fresh_from_zero_lineage=false`. They can NEVER be claimed under from-zero
     gates or confused with the L6 run. Slumbot results labeled `bc_bootstrap DIAGNOSTIC`.
  3. GPU contention: BC training is the only GPU-heavy step (~hours). Schedule it in one
     window; accept one day of reduced trainer throughput; log the window in Ops log.
- exact change (4 phases, no trainer change):
  - Phase 0 — TEACHER DATA AUDIT (CPU only). Raw V2/V3 CFR solves were DELETED; the
    surviving presampled sets (`data/training/v3_srp_50bb_sampled/` etc.) are 54-dim
    value-net features and likely CANNOT reconstruct AlphaHoldem obs (full action
    history + exact cards needed). Inventory three candidate teacher paths and report
    feasibility BEFORE building anything:
    (a) 200bb SRP raw solves on disk (`data/cfr/pipeline_v3_hu_srp_200bb/` — QA-passed
        boards only): right stack depth, SRP-only coverage, bucketed strategies.
    (b) Teacher-bot distillation: play the production V10+realtime-resolver bot (our
        strongest system, Pluribus-style search) in the v55 AlphaHoldem env and record
        (obs, action distribution, resolver EV) tuples directly in net format. Measure
        decisions/sec first to size the dataset (target >=5M decisions).
    (c) Presampled 54-dim data: confirm (likely) infeasibility in one paragraph, then drop.
  - Phase 1 — BC dataset build from the chosen path(s). Every sample MUST carry a value
    target (CFR/resolver EV) — this is the RL-1 fix and is non-negotiable.
  - Phase 2 — BC training: fresh AlphaHoldemNet, policy head soft-CE on teacher
    distribution + value head regression on EV, standard early stopping. Save
    `bc_bootstrap_v1.pt` with honest metadata.
  - Phase 3 — evaluation: mirror eval (post-fix script) BC vs V4 anchor and BC vs
    current V5 checkpoint, 10k pairs each; then quick5k vs Slumbot (direct launch path,
    greedy, full artifact bundle, DIAGNOSTIC label).
- gate / decision rules (pre-registered to prevent motivated reading):
  - BC quick5k <= -60 bb/100 AND mirror-eval clearly above current V5: escalate to user
    with a switch proposal (BC -> value-warm RL continuation plan).
  - BC in (-60, -90]: equivocal — from-zero continues; BC model becomes a mirror-eval
    anchor and possible pool seed candidate (separate registration).
  - BC > -90 (i.e., no better than current V5): record lesson, close branch.
- abort criteria: Phase 0 finds no viable teacher path (report and close); Phase 2 BC
  val loss fails to beat trivial baselines; total branch cost exceeding ~4 days of Codex
  time without reaching Phase 3.
- rollback: delete/park the branch dir; main line untouched by construction.
- result / lesson: Phase 0 teacher-data audit completed CPU-only on 2026-07-07; detail
  `reports/v5_exp009_phase0_teacher_data_audit_20260707.md`. Verdict:
  `CONDITIONAL_FEASIBLE_BUT_PHASE1_BLOCKED`. No existing dataset is ready for BC
  because none carries both AlphaHoldem v55 obs/policy targets and value targets.
  The only plausible source is `data/cfr/pipeline_v3_hu_srp_200bb/`, but it currently
  exports policy-only rows (`key`, `probs`). Before Phase 1 dataset build, a CPU-only
  proof must show that value targets can be recovered/exported for sampled 200bb CFR
  nodes; otherwise close EXP-009. Follow-up proof completed at 2026-07-07 07:42 EDT;
  detail `reports/v5_exp009_value_target_proof_20260707.md`. Verdict:
  `FAILED_PHASE1_NO_GO`: current artifacts cannot recover/export the mandatory value
  target, so Phase 1 is closed/no-go for this artifact set. Main from-zero run
  untouched; no BC branch created.

| 2026-07-09 09:05 | FABLE DEEP DIAGNOSIS: V5 play-style from formal100k dump (190,917 hero decisions) | Concrete leak structure, why V5 is stuck at ~-100: PREFLOP fold 56% / RAISE 43% / CALL 0% — pure fold-or-raise, zero flatting (matches loss_report BB-call 0.0%); creates preflop 3bet/4bet/5bet jam wars (the b200→b600→b1200→b5400→b10800 losing lines) → hero_fold bucket -145 bb/100 across 66% of hands (invests via raises then folds off chips to Slumbot re-aggression). POSTFLOP hyper-passive: flop bet 3% (check 79%/call 10%/fold 9%), turn bet 3%, river bet 3% — almost never the aggressor; a passive calling station that check-calls to showdown (-110) and never value-bets/bluffs. ROOT CAUSE = M1 crystallized: noisy value signal (no variance reduction) → critic cannot learn board-texture-dependent postflop betting → policy collapses to the two low-variance-regret actions (coarse preflop polarize + postflop check-call). This is a bad LOCAL OPTIMUM, not an inability to learn — it minimizes luck-driven regret but is strategically hollow, and it is why 200M hands produced no Slumbot progress. EXP-003 SUCCESS CRITERION MADE CONCRETE: after cutover, postflop bet frequency must climb from 3% toward a normal 30-60% aggressor range; if it stays ~3% over ≥50M hands, this run is confirmed stuck → clean restart / CFR-warmstart. |
